<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\AppSetting;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class AppSettingController extends Controller  //Controleur des reglages generaux (points de fidelite, horaires)
{
    public function index()  //affiche la page de configuration Blade et lesvariables de configuration
    {
       $settings = AppSetting::with('updatedBy:id,first_name,last_name')->get(); // Recuperation de tous les parametres avec le nom de l'administrateur qui les a modifies
        return view('admin.settings.index', compact('settings')); // Affichage de la vue de gestion des parametres
    }
    public function update(Request $request, $key)  //Enregistre les nouveaux parametres globaux et mise a jour d'une cle de configuration
    {
         $setting = AppSetting::where('setting_key', $key)->firstOrFail();// Recherche du parametre par sa cle unique dans la table
        $validated = $request->validate([ // Validation de la valeur soumise
                'setting_value' => 'required|string', // La valeur doit etre renseignee sous forme de chaine
        ]);
        $setting->update([  // Mise a jour de la valeur et de l'historique d'auteur
            'setting_value' => $validated['setting_value'],  // Enregistrement de la nouvelle valeur du parametre
            'updated_by'    => auth()->id(), // Tracabilite de l'administrateur qui fait la modification
        ]);
        return redirect()->route('admin.settings.index') // Redirection vers la page de reglages avec confirmation
            ->with('success', "Le paramÃ¨tre '{$key}' a Ã©tÃ© mis Ã  jour.");
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Claim;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ClaimController extends Controller
{
    // Affiche la liste des reclamations dans une vue Blade
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $query = Claim::with([
            'user:id,first_name,last_name,email,phone',
            'order:id,order_number,total_fcfa,created_at',
            'handler:id,first_name,last_name',
        ]);

        if ($request->filled('status')) {
            $query->where('status', $request->status);
        }

        $claims = $query->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get();

        return view('admin.claims.index', compact('claims'));
    }

    // Formulaire pour repondre a une reclamation
    public function edit($id)
    {
        $claim = Claim::with(['user', 'order', 'handler'])->findOrFail($id);

        return view('admin.claims.edit', compact('claim'));
    }

    // Traite la reclamation et sauvegarde la decision
    public function update(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $claim = Claim::findOrFail($id);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status'   => 'required|in:open,in_progress,resolved,rejected',
            'response' => 'required|string',
        ]);

        $claim->update([
            'status'     => $validated['status'],
            'response'   => $validated['response'],
            'handled_by' => auth()->id(),
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.claims.index')
            ->with('success', 'La reclamation a ete mise a jour.');
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\EmployeeController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\Role;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class EmployeeController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $employeeRole = Role::where('code', 'employee')->firstOrFail();
        $query = User::where('role_id', $employeeRole->id);

        $employees = $query->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get();

        return view('admin.employees.index', compact('employees'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return view('admin.employees.create');
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'first_name' => 'required|string|max:80',
            'last_name'  => 'required|string|max:80',
            'email'      => 'required|email|unique:users,email|max:255',
            'phone'      => 'nullable|string|max:20',
            'password'   => ['required', 'string', Password::min(8)->mixedCase()->numbers()],
        ]);

        $employeeRole = Role::where('code', 'employee')->firstOrFail();

        User::create([
            'role_id'       => $employeeRole->id,
            'first_name'    => $validated['first_name'],
            'last_name'     => $validated['last_name'],
            'email'         => $validated['email'],
            'phone'         => $validated['phone'] ?? null,
            'password_hash' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
            'is_active'     => true,
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.employees.index')
            ->with('success', 'Le compte employe a ete cree avec succes.');
    }

    public function edit($id)
    {
        $employee = User::findOrFail($id);

        return view('admin.employees.edit', compact('employee'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $employee = User::findOrFail($id);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'first_name' => 'required|string|max:80',
            'last_name'  => 'required|string|max:80',
            'email'      => 'required|email|max:255|unique:users,email,' . $id,
            'phone'      => 'nullable|string|max:20',
        ]);

        $employee->update([
            'first_name' => $validated['first_name'],
            'last_name'  => $validated['last_name'],
            'email'      => $validated['email'],
            'phone'      => $validated['phone'] ?? null,
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.employees.index')
            ->with('success', 'Le compte employe a ete mis a jour avec succes.');
    }

    public function destroy($id)
    {
        $employee = User::findOrFail($id);
        $employee->delete();

        return redirect()->route('admin.employees.index')
            ->with('success', 'L\'employe a ete supprime.');
    }
}

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\MenuCategory;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;


class MenuCategoryController extends Controller //Classe gerant la logique des categories de menus (Plats, Boissons, etc.)
{
   
    public function index() //Affiche la liste des categories de menu
    {
        $categories = MenuCategory::orderBy('sort_order', 'asc')->get(); // Recuperation de toutes les categories triees par ordre d'affichage croissant
        return view('admin.categories.index', compact('categories')); // Rendu de la vue Blade et transmission de la variable $categories
    }

    public function create() //Affiche le formulaire de creation de categorie
    {
        return view('admin.categories.create');// Rendu du formulaire Blade d'ajout
    }
    
    public function store(Request $request) // Methode de traitement du formulaire de creation et enregistrement d'une nouvelle categorie
    {
        
        $validated = $request->validate([  // Validation des regles pour une categorie
            'name'       => 'required|string|unique:menu_categories,name|max:100', // Nom unique obligatoire
            'sort_order' => 'required|integer|min:0', // Ordre de tri sous forme d'entier
            'is_active'  => 'required|boolean', // Booleen indiquant si la categorie est active
        ]);
       
        MenuCategory::create($validated);  // Enregistrement effectif de la nouvelle categorie dans la base MySQL
        return redirect()->route('admin.categories.index')  // Redirection vers la liste des categories avec un message de succes
            ->with('success', 'Categorie creee avec succes.');
    }
        public function edit($id)// Methode affichant l'ecran d'edition et le formulaire de modification de categorie
    {
        $category = MenuCategory::findOrFail($id); // Recherche de la categorie par son identifiant ou levee d'une erreur 404
        return view('admin.categories.edit', compact('category')); // Chargement de la vue Blade avec l'objet categorie
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id)  //Methode enregistrant les modifications/mise a jour apportees a une categorie
    {
        $category = MenuCategory::findOrFail($id); // Recuperation de l'enregistrement existant
        $validated = $request->validate([ // Validation des donnees modifiees
            'name'       => 'required|string|max:100|unique:menu_categories,name,' . $id,  // Verification du nom en ignorant l'enregistrement en cours
            'sort_order' => 'required|integer|min:0', // Ordre de tri valide
            'is_active'  => 'required|boolean',  // Statut actif/inactif
        ]);
        $category->update($validated); // Application des modifications en base de donnees
        return redirect()->route('admin.categories.index') // Redirection vers l'index avec notification
            ->with('success', 'Categorie mise a jour avec succes.');
    }

    public function destroy($id)// Methode de suppression definitive d'une categorie
    {
        $category = MenuCategory::findOrFail($id); // Recherche de la categorie ciblee
        $category->delete(); // Execution de la suppression dans la base de donnees
        return redirect()->route('admin.categories.index')// Redirection avec message de confirmation
            ->with('success', 'Categorie supprimee avec succes.');
    }
}

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\MenuItem;
use App\Models\MenuCategory;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class MenuItemController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)  //Methode d'affichage de la carte des elements du menu
    {
        $query = MenuItem::with('category');// Preparation de la requete avec prechargement de la relation category (evite le probleme SQL N+1)
        if ($request->filled('category_id')) { // Filtrage dynamique si une categorie specifique est selectionnee
            
            $query->where('category_id', $request->category_id); // Ajout du filtre par ID de categorie
        }

       $items = $query->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get();  // Execution de la requete de tri par date
        $categories = MenuCategory::where('is_active', true)->get(); // Recuperation de toutes les categories actives pour alimenter un filtre de recherche
        return view('admin.menu.index', compact('items', 'categories')); // Transmission des elements et categories e la vue Blade
    }

    public function create() // Methode pour afficher le formulaire de creation/d'ajout d'un plat ou d'une boisson.
    {
       $categories = MenuCategory::where('is_active', true)->get(); // Recuperation de la liste des categories actives pour le champ 'select'
        return view('admin.menu.create', compact('categories')); // Affichage de la vue de creation
    }

    public function store(Request $request) // Methode de sauvegarde/Enregistrement d'un nouvel element
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([ // Validation stricte des donnees envoyees par le formulaire
            'category_id'      => 'nullable|exists:menu_categories,id', // ID de categorie optionnel mais devant exister dans la table menu_categories
            'name'             => 'required|string|max:150', // Intitule du plat obligatoire
            'description'      => 'nullable|string',  // Description optionnelle
            'price_fcfa'       => 'required|integer|min:0', // Prix en FCFA obligatoire et positif
            'image_url'        => 'nullable|url|max:500',// Lien vers l'image valide comme une URL valide
            'is_available'     => 'boolean',   // Disponibilite (booleen)
            'is_daily_special' => 'boolean', // Indicateur du plat du jour (booleen)
        ]);

        // Conversion du champ case a cocher en valeur booleenne explicite
        $validated['is_available'] = $request->has('is_available');

        // Conversion de la case du plat du jour en booleen
        $validated['is_daily_special'] = $request->has('is_daily_special');
        
        if ($validated['is_daily_special']) {   // Regle metier : S'il s'agit du plat du jour, desactiver l'ancien plat du jour unique
        MenuItem::where('is_daily_special', true)->update(['is_daily_special' => false]); // Remise e zero de tous les autres plats du jour
        }
        MenuItem::create($validated); // Creation de l'enregistrement en BDD 
        return redirect()->route('admin.menu.index') // Redirection vers le tableau du menu
            ->with('success', 'L\'element du menu a ete ajoute avec succes.');
    }

    public function edit($id) //Affiche le formulaire d'edition d'un plat.
    {
        
        $item = MenuItem::findOrFail($id);// Recuperation de l'article par son ID
        $categories = MenuCategory::where('is_active', true)->get(); // Recuperation des categories actives

        // Rendu de la vue avec injection de l'article et des categories
        return view('admin.menu.edit', compact('item', 'categories'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id)  // Methode d'enregistrement des modifications/mise a jour d'un plat
    {
        
        $item = MenuItem::findOrFail($id);// Recuperation de l'enregistrement cible
        $validated = $request->validate([// Validation des modifications
           
            'category_id'      => 'nullable|exists:menu_categories,id', // Validation de la categorie
            'name'             => 'required|string|max:150',// Validation du nom du plat
            'description'      => 'nullable|string',// Validation de la description
            'price_fcfa'       => 'required|integer|min:0', // Validation du prix en FCFA
            'image_url'        => 'nullable|url|max:500', // Validation de l'URL de l'image
            'is_available'     => 'boolean', // Booleen disponibilite
            'is_daily_special' => 'boolean',// Booleen plat du jour
        ]);

        $validated['is_available'] = $request->has('is_available'); // Gestion du statut disponible via la checkbox
        $validated['is_daily_special'] = $request->has('is_daily_special'); // Gestion du statut du plat du jour via la checkbox
        if ($validated['is_daily_special']) { // Si coche comme plat du jour, decocher tous les autres plats sauf celui-ci 
            MenuItem::where('id', '<>', $id)->update(['is_daily_special' => false]); // Mise a jour de la table pour reinitialiser les autres plats
        }
        $item->update($validated); // Sauvegarde des changements
        return redirect()->route('admin.menu.index')// Redirection vers le menu
            ->with('success', 'L\'article du menu a ete mis a jour.');
    }

    public function destroy($id) //Supprime un article du menu.
    {
       $item = MenuItem::findOrFail($id); // Recherche du plat a supprimer
       $item->delete();// Suppression de la ligne en base de donnees
        return redirect()->route('admin.menu.index') // Redirection vers la liste du menu
        ->with('success', 'L\'article a ete supprime de la carte.');
    }
}

<?php
namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Promotion;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class PromotionController extends Controller// ContrOleur gerant les offres et affiches promotionnelles
{
     public function index() //Affiche la liste des promotions dans la vue Blade. Affichage des promotions existantes
   {
$promotions = Promotion::orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get(); //Recuperation de la liste triee par la date de creation la plus recente
    return view('admin.promotions.index', compact('promotions')); // Envoi des donnees a la vue Blade dediee
    }
        public function create()//Formulaire de creation d'une promotion : Methode affichant le formulaire de creation
     {
        return view('admin.promotions.create'); // Renvoie de la vue Blade de creation
    }

    public function store(Request $request)//Enregistre la promotion et redirige : Sauvegarde d'une nouvelle promotion
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([// Validation des regles pour une offre promotionnelle
           'title'       => 'required|string|max:150', // Titre de la promotion obligatoire
            'description' => 'nullable|string',// Description detaillee optionnelle
            'image_url'   => 'nullable|url|max:500',// URL de l'affiche publicitaire
            'starts_at'   => 'nullable|date',// Date de debut d'affichage
            'ends_at'     => 'nullable|date|after_or_equal:starts_at', // Date de fin devant etre posterieure ou egale a la date de debut
            'is_active'   => 'boolean',  // Statut actif
        ]);

        
        $validated['is_active'] = $request->has('is_active');// Affectation du statut actif selon la case a cocher
         Promotion::create($validated); // Creation de la promotion en base de donnees
        return redirect()->route('admin.promotions.index') // Redirection vers l'index des promotions
            ->with('success', 'La promotion a ete enregistree.');
    }
   
    public function edit($id) // Formulaire d'eedition de la promotion : Methode de preparation de l'edition
    {
        $promotion = Promotion::findOrFail($id);// Recherche de la promotion ciblee
        return view('admin.promotions.edit', compact('promotion')); // Rendu du formulaire de modification avec les donnees
    }
       
    public function update(Request $request, $id) //Met a jour une promotion.Sauvegarde des donnees modifiees
    {
        $promotion = Promotion::findOrFail($id);// Recuperation de l'enregistrement a mettre a jour
        $validated = $request->validate([// Validation des champs modifies
            
            'title'       => 'required|string|max:150',// Titre requis
            'description' => 'nullable|string',// Description
            'image_url'   => 'nullable|url|max:500', // URL d'image validee
            'starts_at'   => 'nullable|date',// Date de debut
            'ends_at'     => 'nullable|date|after_or_equal:starts_at',// Date de fin coherente
            'is_active'   => 'boolean', // Case a cocher actif/inactif
        ]);

        $validated['is_active'] = $request->has('is_active');// Mise a jour de la valeur booleenne d'activation
        $promotion->update($validated); // Application de la mise a jour
        return redirect()->route('admin.promotions.index')  // Redirection avec confirmation
            ->with('success', 'La promotion a ete mise a jour.');
    }

    public function destroy($id)//Supprime une promotion/une offre
    {
        $promotion = Promotion::findOrFail($id);// Recherche de la promotion par ID
        $promotion->delete(); // Suppression de la base de donnees
        return redirect()->route('admin.promotions.index')// Redirection vers la liste des promotions
            ->with('success', 'La promotion a Ã©tÃ© supprimÃ©e.');
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\LoginController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class LoginController extends Controller
{
    // affiche le formulaire de connexion
    public function create() {
        return view('auth.login');
    }

    /* valide les identifiants et utilise Auth::attempt()
    si Ã§a Ã©choue, une Ã©rreur est renvoyÃ©e
    si elle rÃ©ussie, la session est regÃ©nÃ©rÃ©e et l'utilisateur se dirige vers le tableau de bord */
    public function store(Request $request) {

        $credentials = $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ]);

        if (! Auth::attempt($credentials, $request->boolean('remember'))) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'email' => "Identifiants incorrects.",
            ])->onlyInput('email');
        }

        $request->session()->regenerate();
        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard'));
    }

    /*dÃ©connecter l'utilisateur, invalide la session
    et regÃ©nÃ¨re un jeton de sÃ©curitÃ© CSRF  */
    public function destroy(Request $request) {
        Auth::logout();
        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken(); 
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordResetController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;

class PasswordResetController extends Controller
{
    // affiche le formulaire "mot de passe oubliÃ©"
    public function request() {
        return view('auth.forgot-password');
    }

    // valide l"email et renvoie le lien de rÃ©initialisation
    public function email(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate(["email" => ['required', 'string', 'max:255', 'email']]);
        $status = Password::sendResetLink($request->only('email'));

        return $status === Password::RESET_LINK_SENT
            ? back()->with('status', __($status))
            : back()->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }

    // affiche le formulaire pour crÃ©er un nouveau mot de passe,
    //en passant le token et l'email dans la vue
    public function reset(string $token) {
        return view('auth.reset-password', [
            'token' => $token,
            'email' => request('email'),
        ]);
    }

    /* vÃ©rifie les donnÃ©es, utilise Password::reset()
    si le token est valide, la fonction anonyme met
    Ã  jour le mot de passe de l'utilisateur */
    public function update(Request $request) {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => 'required',
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255', 'email'],
            'password' => Password::defaults(),
        ]);

        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function ($user, $password) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => $password,
                ])->save();
            }
        );

        return $status === Password::PASSWORD_RESET
            ? redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status))
            : back()->withErrors(['email' => [__($status)]]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\LoyaltyAccount;
use App\Models\MenuItem;
use App\Models\Promotion;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class AccueilController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        // Solde de points affichÃ© dans le bandeau ("12 pts")
        $balance = (int) LoyaltyAccount::whereKey($user->id)->value('balance_points');

        // Menu du jour : 4 plats disponibles marquÃ©s "plat du jour"
        $dailySpecials = MenuItem::where('is_available', true)
            ->where('is_daily_special', true)
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->limit(4)
            ->get();

        // Promotions en cours : actives et dont les dates encadrent aujourd'hui
        // (une date NULL = pas de limite)
        $promotions = Promotion::where('is_active', true)
            ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull('starts_at')->orWhere('starts_at', '<=', now()))
            ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull('ends_at')->orWhere('ends_at', '>=', now()))
            ->latest('created_at')
            ->limit(2)
            ->get();

        // Top 10 des clients : ?periode=semaine | mois | tout
        $periode = in_array($request->query('periode'), ['semaine', 'mois'], true)
            ? $request->query('periode') : 'tout';

        $depuis = match ($periode) {
            'semaine' => now()->subWeek(),
            'mois'    => now()->subMonth(),
            default   => null,
        };

        $topClients = User::whereHas('role', fn ($q) => $q->where('code', 'student'))
            // withCount crÃ©e la colonne "commandes_terminees" sans charger les commandes
            ->withCount(['orders as commandes_terminees' => function ($q) use ($depuis) {
                $q->where('status', 'completed');
                if ($depuis) {
                    $q->where('created_at', '>=', $depuis);
                }
            }])
            ->having('commandes_terminees', '>', 0)
            ->orderByDesc('commandes_terminees')
            ->orderBy('first_name')               // dÃ©partage les ex-aequo
            ->limit(10)
            ->get(['id', 'first_name', 'last_name']); // jamais d'email des autres Ã©tudiants

        return view('etudiant.accueil', compact('user', 'balance', 'dailySpecials', 'promotions', 'topClients', 'periode'));
    }

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Etudiant\CartController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\MenuItem;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Session;

class CartController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Affiche le contenu du panier.
     */
    public function index()
    {
        $cart = Session::get('cart', []);
        $total = $this->calculateTotal($cart);

        return view('etudiant.cart.index', compact('cart', 'total'));
    }

    /**
     * Ajoute un plat au panier (AppelÃ© via AJAX ou formulaire).
     */
    public function add(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'menu_item_id' => 'required|exists:menu_items,id',
            'quantity' => 'required|integer|min:1'
        ]);

        $item = MenuItem::findOrFail($request->menu_item_id);
        
        if (!$item->is_available) {
            return back()->with('error', 'DÃ©solÃ©, ce plat est actuellement Ã©puisÃ©.');
        }

        $cart = Session::get('cart', []);

        // Si le plat est dÃ©jÃ  dans le panier, on incrÃ©mente la quantitÃ©
        if (isset($cart[$item->id])) {
            $cart[$item->id]['quantity'] += $request->quantity;
        } else {
            // Sinon, on l'ajoute
            $cart[$item->id] = [
                'id' => $item->id,
                'name' => $item->name,
                'price_fcfa' => $item->price_fcfa,
                'quantity' => $request->quantity,
                'image_url' => $item->image_url,
            ];
        }

        Session::put('cart', $cart);

        return back()->with('success', $item->name . ' a Ã©tÃ© ajoutÃ© Ã  votre miam miam panier !');
    }

    /**
     * Diminue la quantitÃ© d'un article ou le retire du panier.
     */
    public function remove(Request $request)
    {
        $cart = Session::get('cart', []);
        $id = $request->item_id;

        if (isset($cart[$id])) {
            if ($cart[$id]['quantity'] > 1 && !$request->boolean('force_delete')) {
                $cart[$id]['quantity'] -= 1;
            } else {
                unset($cart[$id]);
            }
            Session::put('cart', $cart);
        }

        return back()->with('success', 'Panier mis Ã  jour.');
    }

    /**
     * Calcule le total du panier en FCFA.
     */
    private function calculateTotal(array $cart): int
    {
        $total = 0;
        foreach ($cart as $item) {
            $total += $item['price_fcfa'] * $item['quantity'];
        }
        return $total;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\CookieConsent;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class CookieController extends Controller
{
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate(['consented' => ['required', 'boolean']]);

        CookieConsent::create([
            'user_id'        => $request->user()?->id,
            'session_id'     => $request->user() ? null : $request->session()->getId(),
            'consented'      => $request->boolean('consented'),
            'policy_version' => '1.0',
        ]);

        $request->session()->put('cookie_consent_answered', true);

        return back();
    }
}

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

class MenuController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Affiche la page Commander : Plats, catÃ©gories, promotions et panier intÃ©grÃ©.
     */
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $user = $request->user();
        $balance = (int) LoyaltyAccount::whereKey($user->id)->value('balance_points');

        // 1. RÃ©cupÃ©rer les promotions actives
        $promotions = Promotion::where('is_active', true)
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->whereNull('starts_at')->orWhere('starts_at', '<=', now());
            })
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->whereNull('ends_at')->orWhere('ends_at', '>=', now());
            })
            ->get();

        // 2. RÃ©cupÃ©rer les plats du jour disponibles
        $dailySpecials = MenuItem::where('is_daily_special', true)
            ->where('is_available', true)
            ->get();

        // 3. RÃ©cupÃ©rer le menu classÃ© par catÃ©gories (utilise la relation 'items' de MenuCategory)
        $categories = MenuCategory::with(['items' => function ($query) {
                $query->where('is_available', true);
            }])
            ->where('is_active', true)
            ->orderBy('sort_order', 'asc')
            ->get();

        // 4. RÃ©cupÃ©rer le panier en session pour l'afficher en bas de page
        $cart = Session::get('cart', []);

        return view('etudiant.menu.index', compact(
            'user',
            'balance',
            'promotions',
            'dailySpecials',
            'categories',
            'cart'
        ));
    }

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Etudiant\OrderController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Order;
use App\Models\OrderItem;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Session;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class OrderController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Valide le panier et crÃ©e la commande en base de donnÃ©es.
     */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $cart = Session::get('cart', []);

        if (empty($cart)) {
            return redirect()->route('etudiant.cart.index')->with('error', 'Votre panier est vide.');
        }

        // Validation du choix de l'Ã©tudiant (Livraison ou sur place)
        $request->validate([
            'service_mode' => 'required|in:delivery,on_site',
            'delivery_address' => 'required_if:service_mode,delivery'
        ]);

        $subtotal = 0;
        foreach ($cart as $item) {
            $subtotal += $item['price_fcfa'] * $item['quantity'];
        }

        // Utilisation de DB::transaction pour s'assurer que tout s'enregistre, ou rien du tout en cas d'erreur
        DB::transaction(function () use ($cart, $request, $subtotal) {
            $user = Auth::user();

            // 1. CrÃ©ation de l'entÃªte de la commande
            $order = Order::create([
                'order_number' => 'CMD-' . strtoupper(Str::random(6)),
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'service_mode' => $request->service_mode,
                'delivery_address' => $request->service_mode === 'delivery' ? $request->delivery_address : null,
                'subtotal_fcfa' => $subtotal,
                'total_fcfa' => $subtotal, // Le calcul de la rÃ©duction par points viendra s'ajouter ici plus tard
                'status' => 'pending'
            ]);

            // 2. Figer les articles et leurs prix dans l'historique
            foreach ($cart as $item) {
                OrderItem::create([
                    'order_id' => $order->id,
                    'menu_item_id' => $item['id'],
                    'item_name' => $item['name'],
                    'unit_price_fcfa' => $item['price_fcfa'],
                    'quantity' => $item['quantity'],
                    'line_total_fcfa' => $item['price_fcfa'] * $item['quantity'],
                ]);
            }

            // 3. Calcul automatique des points de fidÃ©litÃ© gagnÃ©s (1 point pour 1000 FCFA)
            $pointsEarned = floor($subtotal / 1000);
            if ($pointsEarned > 0) {
                $order->update(['loyalty_points_earned' => $pointsEarned]);
            }
        });

        // 4. On vide le panier une fois la commande enregistrÃ©e
        Session::forget('cart');

        return redirect()->route('etudiant.menu.index')->with('success', 'Votre commande a Ã©tÃ© envoyÃ©e en cuisine !');
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Controllers\Etudiant\ParrainageController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Etudiant;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Referral;
use App\Models\ReferralCode;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class ParrainageController extends Controller
{
    // Alphabet sans 0/O ni 1/I pour Ã©viter les erreurs de saisie
    private const ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';

    /** POST : bouton "GÃ©nÃ©rer un nouveau code" */
    public function generer(Request $request)
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        // On DÃ‰SACTIVE l'ancien code au lieu de le supprimer :
        // les parrainages dÃ©jÃ  faits pointent encore vers lui (clÃ© Ã©trangÃ¨re)
        ReferralCode::where('user_id', $user->id)->where('is_active', true)->update(['is_active' => false]);

        // PrÃ©fixe = 5 premiÃ¨res lettres du prÃ©nom sans accents (Ã‰lodie -> ELODI)
        $prefixe = substr(preg_replace('/[^A-Z]/', '', strtoupper(Str::ascii($user->first_name))), 0, 5) ?: 'MIAM';

        do {
            $suffixe = '';
            for ($i = 0; $i < 3; $i++) {
                $suffixe .= self::ALPHABET[random_int(0, strlen(self::ALPHABET) - 1)];
            }
            $code = "$prefixe-$suffixe";                       // ex : MARIE-7K2
        } while (ReferralCode::where('code', $code)->exists()); // colonne code = UNIQUE

        ReferralCode::create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'code' => $code, 'is_active' => true]);

        return back()->with('success', "Votre nouveau code est $code.");
    }

    /** POST : saisir le code de SON parrain */
    public function utiliser(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate(['code' => ['required', 'string', 'max:30']]);
        $user = $request->user();

        $code = ReferralCode::where('code', strtoupper(trim($request->code)))
            ->where('is_active', true)->first();

        // Chaque rÃ¨gle bloque une fraude possible
        $erreur = match (true) {
            ! $code                                                   => "Ce code n'existe pas ou n'est plus actif.",
            $code->user_id === $user->id                              => 'Vous ne pouvez pas utiliser votre propre code.',
            Referral::where('referred_user_id', $user->id)->exists() => 'Vous avez dÃ©jÃ  un parrain.',
            $user->orders()->exists()                                 => 'Un code ne peut Ãªtre utilisÃ© quâ€™avant votre premiÃ¨re commande.',
            default                                                   => null,
        };

        if ($erreur) {
            return back()->withErrors(['code' => $erreur]);
        }

        // Le parrain sera rÃ©compensÃ© quand la 1re commande du filleul sera "completed"
        Referral::create([
            'referrer_user_id' => $code->user_id,
            'referred_user_id' => $user->id,
            'code_id'          => $code->id,
            'status'           => 'pending',
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Code de parrainage enregistrÃ©.');
    }
}

     */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {

            'description' => 'required|string'
        ]);

        // SÃ©curitÃ© : VÃ©rifier que la commande appartient bien Ã  l'Ã©tudiant connectÃ©

            ->where('user_id', Auth::id())

        Claim::create([
            'order_id'    => $order->id,
            'user_id'     => Auth::id(),
            'subject'     => $request->subject ?: 'ProblÃ¨me sur la commande #' . $order->order_number,
            'description' => $request->description,
            'status'      => 'open'
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('etudiant.reclamations.index')
            ->with('success', 'Votre rÃ©clamation a Ã©tÃ© transmise avec succÃ¨s aux employÃ©s.');
    }

<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

// "auth" vÃ©rifie QUI vous Ãªtes ; "role" vÃ©rifie CE QUE vous avez le droit de faire
class EnsureRole
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next, string ...$roles): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        abort_if(! $user || ! $user->is_active, 403, 'Compte dÃ©sactivÃ©.');
        abort_unless(in_array($user->role?->code, $roles, true), 403, "Vous n'avez pas accÃ¨s Ã  cette page.");

        return $next($request);
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Http\Requests\StoreUserRequest.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;
use Override;

class StoreUserRequest extends FormRequest
{
    /**
     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
     */
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
     *
     * @return array<string, ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
     */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'role_id' => [
                'required',
                'integer',
                Rule::exists('roles', 'id'),
                Rule::in([1, 2, 3, 4]),
            ],
            'first_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:80'],
            'last_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:80'],
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255', 'unique:users,email'],
            'phone' => ['required', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Password::defaults()],
            'delivery_address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'is_active' => ['sometimes', 'boolean'],
        ];
    }

    #[Override]
    // traduis un role textuel en role numÃ©rique
    protected function prepareForValidation()
    {
        if (is_string($this->role_id)) {
            $map = [
                "etudiant" => 1,
                "employe" => 2,
                "gerant" => 3,
                "administrateur" => 4,
            ];
            $key = strtolower($this->role_id);
            if (isset($map[$key])) {
                $this->merge(["role_id" => $map[$key]]);
            }
        }
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasOne;

class Order extends Model
{
    protected $fillable = [
        'order_number', 'user_id', 'service_mode', 'status', 
        'delivery_address', 'arrival_at', 'customer_note',
        'subtotal_fcfa', 'discount_fcfa', 'delivery_fee_fcfa', 
        'total_fcfa', 'loyalty_points_used', 'loyalty_points_earned',
        'completed_at'
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'arrival_at' => 'datetime',
        'completed_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    public function user(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class);
    }

    public function items(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(OrderItem::class);
    }

    public function payment(): HasOne
    {
        return $this->hasOne(Payment::class);
    }

    public function loyaltyTransactions(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(LoyaltyTransaction::class);
    }

    public function claims(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Claim::class);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasOne;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsToMany;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;

class User extends Authenticatable

{
    use HasFactory, Notifiable; 

    protected $fillable = [
        'role_id', 'first_name', 'last_name', 'email', 
        'phone', 'password', 'delivery_address', 'is_active'
    ];

    protected $hidden = ['password'];

    protected $casts = [
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
        'password' => 'hashed',
    ];

    // Relations
    public function role(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Role::class);
    }

    public function loyaltyAccount(): HasOne
    {
        return $this->hasOne(LoyaltyAccount::class);
    }

    public function referralCode(): HasOne
    {
        return $this->hasOne(ReferralCode::class);
    }

    public function orders(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Order::class);
    }

    public function loyaltyTransactions(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(LoyaltyTransaction::class);
    }

    public function claims(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Claim::class);
    }

    // RÃ©fÃ©rences (parrainages)
    public function referralsMade(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Referral::class, 'referrer_user_id');
    }

    public function referralReceived(): HasOne
    {
        return $this->hasOne(Referral::class, 'referred_user_id');
    }
}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\app\Providers\AppServiceProvider.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Providers;

use Illuminate\Support\ServiceProvider;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class AppServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider
{
    /**
     * Register any application services.
     */
    public function register(): void
    {
        //
    }

    /**
     * Bootstrap any application services.
     */
    // les rÃ¨gles par dÃ©faut pour le mot de passe est dÃ©fini ici
    public function boot(): void
    {
        Password::defaults(function () {
            return Password::min(8)
                ->letters()
                ->numbers()
                ->mixedCase();
        });
    }
}

<?php

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Exceptions;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Middleware;

return Application::configure(basePath: dirname(__DIR__))
    ->withRouting(
        web: __DIR__.'/../routes/web.php',
        commands: __DIR__.'/../routes/console.php',
        health: '/up',
    )
    ->withMiddleware(function (Middleware $middleware): void {
        // DÃ©clare l'alias "role" utilisÃ© dans routes/web.php : ->middleware('role:student')
        $middleware->alias([
            'role' => \App\Http\Middleware\EnsureRole::class,
        ]);
    })
    ->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $exceptions): void {
        //
    })->create();

<?php

use App\Models\User;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Defaults
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option defines the default authentication "guard" and password
    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    |
    */

    'defaults' => [
        'guard' => env('AUTH_GUARD', 'web'),
        'passwords' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_BROKER', 'users'),
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    | which utilizes session storage plus the Eloquent user provider.
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | Supported: "session"
    |
    */

    'guards' => [
        'web' => [
            'driver' => 'session',
            'provider' => 'users',
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User Providers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    | providers to represent the model / table. These providers may then
    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    |
    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    |
    */

    'providers' => [
        'users' => [
            'driver' => 'eloquent',
            'model' => env('AUTH_MODEL', User::class),
        ],

        // 'users' => [
        //     'driver' => 'database',
        //     'table' => 'users',
        // ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Resetting Passwords
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | These configuration options specify the behavior of Laravel's password
    | reset functionality, including the table utilized for token storage
    | and the user provider that is invoked to actually retrieve users.
    |
    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    |
    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    |
    */

    'passwords' => [
        'users' => [
            'provider' => 'users',
            'table' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TABLE', 'password_reset_tokens'),
            'expire' => 60,
            'throttle' => 60,
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Password Confirmation Timeout
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may define the number of seconds before a password confirmation
    | window expires and users are asked to re-enter their password via the
    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
    |
    */

    'password_timeout' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_TIMEOUT', 10800),

];

        ],

    'deprecations' => [

    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

            'emoji' => env('LOG_SLACK_EMOJI', ':boom:'),
            'level' => env('LOG_LEVEL', 'critical'),
            'replace_placeholders' => true,
        ],

        'papertrail' => [
            'driver' => 'monolog',
            'level' => env('LOG_LEVEL', 'debug'),
            'handler' => env('LOG_PAPERTRAIL_HANDLER', SyslogUdpHandler::class),
            'handler_with' => [

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Session Driver
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines the default session driver that is utilized for
    | incoming requests. Laravel supports a variety of storage options to
    | persist session data. Database storage is a great default choice.
    |
    | Supported: "file", "cookie", "database", "memcached",
    |            "redis", "dynamodb", "array"
    |
    */

    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'database'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Lifetime
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session
    | to be allowed to remain idle before it expires. If you want them
    | to expire immediately when the browser is closed then you may
    | indicate that via the expire_on_close configuration option.
    |
    */

    'lifetime' => (int) env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),

    'expire_on_close' => env('SESSION_EXPIRE_ON_CLOSE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Encryption
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option allows you to easily specify that all of your session data
    | should be encrypted before it's stored. All encryption is performed
    | automatically by Laravel and you may use the session like normal.
    |
    */

    'encrypt' => env('SESSION_ENCRYPT', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session File Location
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When utilizing the "file" session driver, the session files are placed
    | on disk. The default storage location is defined here; however, you
    | are free to provide another location where they should be stored.
    |
    */

    'files' => storage_path('framework/sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Connection
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" or "redis" session drivers, you may specify a
    | connection that should be used to manage these sessions. This should
    | correspond to a connection in your database configuration options.
    |
    */

    'connection' => env('SESSION_CONNECTION'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Table
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" session driver, you may specify the table to
    | be used to store sessions. Of course, a sensible default is defined
    | for you; however, you're welcome to change this to another table.
    |
    */

    'table' => env('SESSION_TABLE', 'sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cache Store
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using one of the framework's cache driven session backends, you may
    | define the cache store which should be used to store the session data
    | between requests. This must match one of your defined cache stores.
    |
    | Affects: "dynamodb", "memcached", "redis"
    |
    */

    'store' => env('SESSION_STORE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Sweeping Lottery
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Some session drivers must manually sweep their storage location to get
    | rid of old sessions from storage. Here are the chances that it will
    | happen on a given request. By default, the odds are 2 out of 100.
    |
    */

    'lottery' => [2, 100],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may change the name of the session cookie that is created by
    | the framework. Typically, you should not need to change this value
    | since doing so does not grant a meaningful security improvement.
    |
    */

    'cookie' => env(
        'SESSION_COOKIE',
        Str::slug((string) env('APP_NAME', 'laravel')).'-session'
    ),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Path
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | The session cookie path determines the path for which the cookie will
    | be regarded as available. Typically, this will be the root path of
    | your application, but you're free to change this when necessary.
    |
    */

    'path' => env('SESSION_PATH', '/'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Domain
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value determines the domain and subdomains the session cookie is
    | available to. By default, the cookie will be available to the root
    | domain without subdomains. Typically, this shouldn't be changed.
    |
    */

    'domain' => env('SESSION_DOMAIN'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTPS Only Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | By setting this option to true, session cookies will only be sent back
    | to the server if the browser has a HTTPS connection. This will keep
    | the cookie from being sent to you when it can't be done securely.
    |
    */

    'secure' => env('SESSION_SECURE_COOKIE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTP Access Only
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will prevent JavaScript from accessing the
    | value of the cookie and the cookie will only be accessible through
    | the HTTP protocol. It's unlikely you should disable this option.
    |
    */

    'http_only' => env('SESSION_HTTP_ONLY', true),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Same-Site Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines how your cookies behave when cross-site requests
    | take place, and can be used to mitigate CSRF attacks. By default, we
    | will set this value to "lax" to permit secure cross-site requests.
    |
    | See: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Headers/Set-Cookie#samesitesamesite-value
    |
    | Supported: "lax", "strict", "none", null
    |
    */

    'same_site' => env('SESSION_SAME_SITE', 'lax'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Partitioned Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will tie the cookie to the top-level site for
    | a cross-site context. Partitioned cookies are accepted by the browser
    | when flagged "secure" and the Same-Site attribute is set to "none".
    |
    */

    'partitioned' => env('SESSION_PARTITIONED_COOKIE', false),

];

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\database\factories\UserFactory.php ===
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * @extends Factory<User>
 */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    /**
     * The current password being used by the factory.
     */
    protected static ?string $password;

    /**
     * Define the model's default state.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'email_verified_at' => now(),
            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
     */
    public function unverified(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ]);
    }
}

<script>
    function copyReferralCode() {
        const code = document.getElementById('referralCodeText').innerText.trim();
        if (code && code !== 'AUCUN CODE') {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
            alert('Code ' + code + ' copiÃ© dans le presse-papier !');
        }
    }
</script>
@endsection

<script>
    const subtotalFcfa = {{ $subtotal }};
    function toggleLoyaltyDiscount(usePoints) {
        const discountEl = document.getElementById('discountDisplay');
        const totalEl = document.getElementById('totalDisplay');
        const hiddenInput = document.getElementById('useLoyaltyInput');

        const discount = usePoints ? Math.min(1000, subtotalFcfa) : 0;
        const finalTotal = Math.max(0, subtotalFcfa - discount);

        hiddenInput.value = usePoints ? '1' : '0';
        discountEl.textContent = discount.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' F';
        totalEl.textContent = finalTotal.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' F';
    }
</script>

        {{-- Message d'erreur gÃ©nÃ©ral (->with('error', ...)) --}}
        @if(session('error'))
            <div class="alert alert-danger alert-dismissible fade show" role="alert">
                <i class="bi bi-exclamation-circle me-2"></i>{{ session('error') }}
                <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="alert" aria-label="Fermer"></button>
            </div>
        @endif

        {{-- Erreur "restaurant fermÃ©" (clÃ© "hours" ajoutÃ©e par StoreOrderRequest) --}}
        @error('hours')
            <div class="alert alert-warning">
                <i class="bi bi-clock me-2"></i>{{ $message }}
            </div>
        @enderror

        {{-- C'est ici que s'insÃ¨re le contenu de chaque page (accueil, commander...) --}}
        @yield('content')
    </main>

        <div class="container d-flex flex-column flex-md-row justify-content-between gap-1">

    @unless(session('cookie_consent_answered'))

                    {{-- Deux petits formulaires POST : consented=0 (refuser) ou consented=1 (accepter) --}}

=== FICHIER : C:\Users\SOP\Documents\X2\Projet\developpement web\creation_tables.sql ===
CREATE TABLE users (
  id              INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  role            ENUM('student','employee','manager','admin') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'student',
  full_name       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  email           VARCHAR(190) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  phone           VARCHAR(20),
  password_hash   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  address         VARCHAR(255),                 -- default delivery location
  referral_code   VARCHAR(12) UNIQUE,           -- generated for students
  points_balance  INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,       -- cache of loyalty_ledger
  is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at      DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE menu_items (
  id            INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  category      VARCHAR(50),                    -- plat, boisson, ...
  name          VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  description   TEXT,
  price_fcfa    INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  image_url     VARCHAR(255),
  is_available  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,  -- "plat Ã©puisÃ©"
  daily_date    DATE DEFAULT (CURDATE())  -- "menu du jour"
);

CREATE TABLE promotions (                       -- promos, events, mini-jeux
  id           INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  type         ENUM('promo','event','game') NOT NULL,
  title        VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  description  TEXT,
  poster_url   VARCHAR(255),
  starts_at    DATETIME NOT NULL,
  ends_at      DATETIME NOT NULL,
  is_active    BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  INDEX idx_promo_dates (starts_at, ends_at)
);

CREATE TABLE orders (
  id               INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id          INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  fulfillment      ENUM('dine_in','delivery') NOT NULL,
  arrival_time     DATETIME NULL,                   -- dine_in
  delivery_address VARCHAR(255) NULL,           -- delivery
  status           ENUM('pending','confirmed','preparing','ready','delivered','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  subtotal_fcfa    INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  discount_fcfa    INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_fcfa       INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  points_used      INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  points_earned    INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  comment          TEXT NULL,                   -- left after delivery
  created_at       DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  INDEX idx_orders_created (created_at),
  INDEX idx_orders_user (user_id, created_at),
  INDEX idx_orders_status (status)
);

CREATE TABLE order_items (
  id              INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id        INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  menu_item_id    INT UNSIGNED NULL,            -- NULL if item deleted later
  name_snapshot   VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  unit_price_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  quantity        SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (menu_item_id) REFERENCES menu_items(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE TABLE payments (
  id            INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id      INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  provider      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,           -- cinetpay, stripe, ...
  provider_ref  VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE,            -- transaction id from aggregator
  amount_fcfa   INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  status        ENUM('pending','success','failed','refunded') NOT NULL,
  created_at    DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id)
);

CREATE TABLE loyalty_ledger (
  id          INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id     INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  order_id    INT UNSIGNED NULL,
  points      INT NOT NULL,                     -- positive = earned, negative = spent/expired
  reason      ENUM('order','redeem','referral_reward','manual','expiration') NOT NULL,
  expires_at  DATETIME NULL,                    -- set on earned points (e.g. +12 months)
  created_at  DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id),
  INDEX idx_ledger_user (user_id),
  INDEX idx_ledger_expiry (expires_at)
);

CREATE TABLE referrals (
  id           INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  referrer_id  INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  referee_id   INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL UNIQUE,    -- one referrer per student
  status       ENUM('pending','rewarded') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  rewarded_at  DATETIME NULL,
  created_at   DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (referrer_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (referee_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);

CREATE TABLE complaints (
  id          INT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id     INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  order_id    INT UNSIGNED NULL,
  subject     VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  message     TEXT NOT NULL,
  status      ENUM('open','answered','closed') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
  response    TEXT NULL,
  handled_by  INT UNSIGNED NULL,                -- employee/manager who answered
  created_at  DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at  DATETIME ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id),
  FOREIGN KEY (handled_by) REFERENCES users(id)
);

CREATE TABLE settings (                         -- opening hours, points rate, expiry, ...
  `key`  VARCHAR(60) PRIMARY KEY,
  value  TEXT NOT NULL
);

## About Laravel

Laravel is a web application framework with expressive, elegant syntax. We believe development must be an enjoyable and creative experience to be truly fulfilling. Laravel takes the pain out of development by easing common tasks used in many web projects, such as:

- [Simple, fast routing engine](https://laravel.com/docs/routing).
- [Powerful dependency injection container](https://laravel.com/docs/container).
- Multiple back-ends for [session](https://laravel.com/docs/session) and [cache](https://laravel.com/docs/cache) storage.
- Expressive, intuitive [database ORM](https://laravel.com/docs/eloquent).
- Database agnostic [schema migrations](https://laravel.com/docs/migrations).
- [Robust background job processing](https://laravel.com/docs/queues).
- [Real-time event broadcasting](https://laravel.com/docs/broadcasting).

Laravel is accessible, powerful, and provides tools required for large, robust applications.

## Learning Laravel

Laravel has the most extensive and thorough [documentation](https://laravel.com/docs) and video tutorial library of all modern web application frameworks, making it a breeze to get started with the framework. You can also check out [Laravel Learn](https://laravel.com/learn), where you will be guided through building a modern Laravel application.

If you don't feel like reading, [Laracasts](https://laracasts.com) can help. Laracasts contains thousands of video tutorials on a range of topics including Laravel, modern PHP, unit testing, and JavaScript. Boost your skills by digging into our comprehensive video library.

Thank you for considering contributing to the Laravel framework! The contribution guide can be found in the [Laravel documentation](https://laravel.com/docs/contributions).

-- Mon Miam Miam â€” base de donnÃ©es MVP
-- MySQL 8.0+

SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS cookie_consents;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS app_settings;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS claims;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS loyalty_transactions;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS payments;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS order_items;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS orders;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS promotions;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS menu_items;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS menu_categories;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS referrals;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS referral_codes;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS loyalty_accounts;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS users;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS roles;
SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;

CREATE TABLE roles (
    id TINYINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    code VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    label VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

INSERT INTO roles (code, label) VALUES
    ('student', 'Ã‰tudiant'),
    ('employee', 'EmployÃ©'),
    ('manager', 'GÃ©rant'),
    ('admin', 'Administrateur');

CREATE TABLE users (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    role_id TINYINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    phone VARCHAR(30) NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    delivery_address TEXT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_users_role FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(id),
    INDEX idx_users_role_active (role_id, is_active)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE loyalty_accounts (
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED PRIMARY KEY,
    balance_points INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_loyalty_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE referral_codes (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    code VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_referral_code_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE referrals (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    referrer_user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    referred_user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    code_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('pending', 'rewarded', 'cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    rewarded_at TIMESTAMP NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_referrals_referrer FOREIGN KEY (referrer_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_referrals_referred FOREIGN KEY (referred_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_referrals_code FOREIGN KEY (code_id) REFERENCES referral_codes(id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_referrals_not_self CHECK (referrer_user_id <> referred_user_id),
    INDEX idx_referrals_referrer (referrer_user_id),
    INDEX idx_referrals_status (status)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE menu_categories (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    sort_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE menu_items (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    category_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NULL,
    price_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    image_url VARCHAR(500) NULL,
    is_available BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_daily_special BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_menu_item_category FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES menu_categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    INDEX idx_menu_items_available (is_available, category_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE promotions (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NULL,
    image_url VARCHAR(500) NULL,
    starts_at DATETIME NULL,
    ends_at DATETIME NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT chk_promotion_period CHECK (ends_at IS NULL OR starts_at IS NULL OR ends_at > starts_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE orders (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    order_number VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    service_mode ENUM('delivery', 'on_site') NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('pending', 'confirmed', 'preparing', 'ready', 'delivered', 'completed', 'cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    delivery_address TEXT NULL,
    arrival_at DATETIME NULL,
    customer_note TEXT NULL,
    subtotal_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    delivery_fee_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    loyalty_points_used INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    loyalty_points_earned INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    completed_at DATETIME NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_orders_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_delivery_address CHECK (service_mode = 'on_site' OR delivery_address IS NOT NULL),
    INDEX idx_orders_user_created (user_id, created_at),
    INDEX idx_orders_status_created (status, created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE order_items (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    menu_item_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    item_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    unit_price_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    quantity INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    line_total_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_items_order FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_items_menu_item FOREIGN KEY (menu_item_id) REFERENCES menu_items(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    CONSTRAINT chk_order_item_quantity CHECK (quantity > 0),
    INDEX idx_order_items_order (order_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE payments (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    provider VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    provider_transaction_id VARCHAR(150) NULL,
    method ENUM('mobile_money', 'card', 'cash', 'test') NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('pending', 'paid', 'failed', 'refunded') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    amount_fcfa INT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    paid_at DATETIME NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_payments_order FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    UNIQUE KEY uq_provider_transaction (provider, provider_transaction_id),
    INDEX idx_payments_status (status)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE loyalty_transactions (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    order_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    type ENUM('earn', 'redeem', 'referral', 'adjust', 'expire') NOT NULL,
    points INT NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(255) NULL,
    expires_at DATETIME NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_loyalty_tx_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_loyalty_tx_order FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    CONSTRAINT chk_loyalty_points_nonzero CHECK (points <> 0),
    INDEX idx_loyalty_tx_user_created (user_id, created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE claims (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
    subject VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('open', 'in_progress', 'resolved', 'rejected') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
    response TEXT NULL,
    handled_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_claim_order FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_claim_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_claim_handler FOREIGN KEY (handled_by) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    INDEX idx_claims_status_created (status, created_at),
    INDEX idx_claims_user_created (user_id, created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

CREATE TABLE app_settings (
    setting_key VARCHAR(80) PRIMARY KEY,
    setting_value TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_setting_user FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

INSERT INTO app_settings (setting_key, setting_value) VALUES
    ('loyalty_fcfa_per_point', '1000'),
    ('loyalty_points_per_1000_fcfa_discount', '15'),
    ('loyalty_discount_fcfa', '1000'),
    ('restaurant_open', '08:00'),
    ('restaurant_close', '22:00');

CREATE TABLE cookie_consents (
    id BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
    session_id VARCHAR(120) NULL,
    consented BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    policy_version VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_cookie_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_cookie_subject CHECK (user_id IS NOT NULL OR session_id IS NOT NULL),
    INDEX idx_cookie_user_created (user_id, created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB;

DELIMITER $$

CREATE TRIGGER trg_create_loyalty_account
AFTER INSERT ON users
FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO loyalty_accounts (user_id, balance_points) VALUES (NEW.id, 0);
END$$

DELIMITER ;

import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import laravel from 'laravel-vite-plugin';
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';

export default defineConfig({
    plugins: [
        laravel({
            input: ['resources/css/app.css', 'resources/js/app.js'],
            refresh: true,
        }),
        tailwindcss(),
    ],
    server: {
        watch: {
            ignored: ['**/storage/framework/views/**'],
        },
    },
});
